# Farghānī — FULL cumulative selected-locus bilingual review (Stage XIV, 9 November 2026)

This file contains **all 559 individual Arabic–English selected-clause records** from B2007 printed-page labels **18–145**. It is a complete consolidation of the 11 checked-*excerpt* datasets, **not a continuous diplomatic Arabic edition, not 128 fully proofed pages, and not manuscript collation**. The full page-labelled English working translation is in [WORKING_TRANSLATION_18_145.md](WORKING_TRANSLATION_18_145.md) and inherited critical full research master [FULL_CUMULATIVE_CRITICAL_007.md](FULL_CUMULATIVE_CRITICAL_007.md) remains preserved.

**Evidential separation:** **283** excerpt records from directly inspected digitized modern B2007 edited Arabic pp18–60; **276** lower-grade excerpt records from a secondary modern online retranscription on pp61–145 (retyped from modern edition, not a distinct codex). The **10 bounded comparisons** against photographic 1876 printing, five actual print differences, five print agreements, remain separate; **0 medieval manuscript folios/variants independently checked**, **0 source pages fully independently line-proofread**.

**Contents:** Every record below includes Arabic source excerpt, English reviewed rendering, editorial note, source location and grade. A selected excerpt is not continuous Arabic transcription. Page labels reflect modern B2007 and should never be equated with I1876 original printed pages or codex folios.

## B2007 p18 — Digitized modern Arabic edited source (selected excerpts)

### CR001-001 · revise

**Arabic selected excerpt:** الأصل الأول في ذكر رتب الذات وتعيّن الأسماء منها والصفات وذكر كيفية انتشاء الفروع من تلك الأسماء والصفات من أصولها مع تعرّض لشرح الأسماء التسعة والتسعين ، وذكر انتشاء باقي الأسماء منها دقيقها وجليلها

**Reviewed English:** First foundation: the ranks of the Essence, the determination of names and attributes from it, the derivation of their branches from their roots, an account of the ninety-nine names, and the derivation of the other names, subtle and great.

**Editorial decision:** The older English said 'differentiation' for تعيّن; preserve the technical 'determination'.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-002 · annotate

**Arabic selected excerpt:** بِسْمِ اللَّهِ الرَّحْمنِ الرَّحِيمِ نقل عن رسول اللّه صلّى اللّه عليه وسلّم أنه قال : « قال اللّه عزّ وجلّ : كنت كنزا مخفيّا فأحببت أن أعرف ، فخلقت الخلق لأعرف »

**Reviewed English:** In the name of God, the All-merciful, the Especially Merciful. It is transmitted as from the Messenger of God that God said: 'I was a hidden treasure, and I loved to be known, so I created creation in order to be known.'

**Editorial decision:** Farghānī reports an attribution; the 'hidden treasure' saying lacks an established prophetic isnād. Its interpretive importance must not be treated as authentication.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-003 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** اعلم أيّدك اللّه بالفهم الصحيح أنه قد كنى في هذا الحديث الإلهي عن كنه الغيب وإطلاق الذات الأقدس وباطن الهوية الأزلية بالكنز المخفي

**Reviewed English:** Know—may God strengthen you with sound understanding—that the hidden treasure in this divine saying figuratively signifies the innermost reality of the Unseen, the nondelimitation of the most holy Essence, and the inward aspect of eternal Identity.

**Editorial decision:** Keep kunh (inner reality) distinct from its iṭlāq (nondelimitation).

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-004 · revise

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وذلك لملابسة أن الكنز عبارة عن عين مغيب مكنون وسرّ مخزون مشتمل على جواهر عظيمة الجدوى مرغوب فيها

**Reviewed English:** The analogy arises because a treasure is a concealed and guarded thing, a secret held in reserve, comprising precious jewels whose benefits are great and which are much sought after.

**Editorial decision:** The prior paraphrase omitted the explicit 'great benefit' (ʿaẓīmat al-jadwā) and being sought (marghūb fīhā).

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-005 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** ما بين أنفس تصان ويضنّ ويستأثر بها وبين نفيس ، ربما يقع فيه التسامح والتبدّل خصوصا أو عموما

**Reviewed English:** Some of these are so precious that they are guarded, withheld and reserved to their possessor; others are precious but may be dealt with more freely or exchanged, privately or generally.

**Editorial decision:** The second comparison is between anfas and nafīs, degrees of relative preciousness.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-006 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** ومهما أريد إظهاره يعرض أولا عرضا مجملا بحيث يبين الكنز ويظهر لواجده في محله

**Reviewed English:** When it is desired to disclose a treasure, it is first displayed as a whole, so that the treasure becomes evident to its finder in its own place.

**Editorial decision:** Preserve ijmāl (undifferentiated comprehensiveness), not simply a generic presentation.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-007 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** ثم يعرض ثانيا عرضا يميّز فيه بين الأنفس والنفيس ، وينقل منه عن محله لحكمة يراها الواجد من إظهار كماله الذي يتضمّن غناءه وإظهار التجمّل والجود ونحو ذلك

**Reviewed English:** It is then shown a second time in a display that differentiates what is most precious from what is merely precious; some is removed from its place according to a wisdom perceived by the finder, to display his perfection, including wealth, adornment, generosity and similar qualities.

**Editorial decision:** A figurative sequence of comprehensive and then differentiated disclosure; not a temporal sequence in the divine Essence.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-008 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وكذلك كنه الجناب الأقدس تعالى وتقدّس وله المثل الأعلى في السماوات والأرض وهو العزيز الحكيم ، بلا نسبة تشبيه وتشكيل وتمثيل إليه تعالى عن ذلك علوّا كبيرا

**Reviewed English:** Likewise with the innermost reality of the most holy Presence—exalted and sanctified is He, to whom belongs the highest similitude in the heavens and earth, the Mighty and Wise—without attributing to Him any likeness, configuration or representation. He is vastly exalted above that.

**Editorial decision:** The anti-anthropomorphic qualification is part of the authorial analogy, not an editorial gloss.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-009 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** فإنه الغيب المكنون والسرّ المصون الذي هو أبطن كل باطن

**Reviewed English:** For He is the hidden Unseen and the protected secret, more inward than every inward thing.

**Editorial decision:** This is a divine negative-theological predication in analogy.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-010 · crux

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وبطون المشتمل على جواهر أسماء الذات التي هي أنفس نفائس الأسماء

**Reviewed English:** His inwardness contains the jewels of the essential names, the most precious of all names.

**Editorial decision:** The digital text has the awkward sequence بطون المشتمل. Translation conveys contextual sense but a print/manuscript reading is needed before fixing grammar.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** true; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

**Historical print note:** I1876 original printed p5, scan 9, reading `وبطون المشتمل`; Both historical printed p5 and digitized Beirut show the difficult wording; leave open for manuscript.. Not a manuscript variant.

### CR001-011 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** التي منها ما يستأثر في مكنون الغيب عنده ، فلا يعلمها إلّا هو ومن ارتفعت بينونته ورفعت كينونته ممن هو أكمل جميع

**Reviewed English:** Among them are names He reserves in the hidden mystery with Him; none knows them except He and one whose separateness and individual selfhood have been lifted away, one of the most perfect among all—

**Editorial decision:** The clause continues across B2007 p.18–19; the wording must not be treated as a finished independent proposition.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/18?lang=ar

## B2007 p19 — Digitized modern Arabic edited source (selected excerpts)

### CR001-012 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** الكمّل في عرض التعيّن والتجلّي الأول ، لتحقيق الكمال الذاتي الأشرف المعني بقوله : « فأحببت أن أعرف »

**Reviewed English:** —the perfected, in the presentation of the first determination and first self-disclosure, for realizing the nobler essential perfection intended by 'I loved to be known'.

**Editorial decision:** Continuation from p18; 'in the presentation' preferred to the slightly ambiguous 'participate in the display'.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-013 · revise

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وهو المتضمّن أيضا نفائس درر أسماء الصفات التي يسمح بتعريفها لكل من يصلح لتشريفها من كل فرد متخلّق ، أو كامل متحقّق

**Reviewed English:** It also contains the precious jewels of the names of attributes, acquaintance with which is permitted to anyone suited to be honored by them, whether one who has adopted their character or a perfected person who has realized them.

**Editorial decision:** Distinguish takhalluq (assuming qualities) from taḥaqquq (realizing them).

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-014 · corrected

**Arabic selected excerpt:** والمحتوي أيضا على لآلىء أسماء الأفعال العامّ نفعها وأثرها ، والمستفيض حكمها وخبرها في جميع المراتب الكونيّة التي هي محال تمام الكمال الأسمائي والمطلب الغائبّ المعني بقوله : « فخلقت الخلق لأعرف »

**Reviewed English:** It also contains the pearls of the names of acts, whose benefits and effects are universal, and whose judgments and reports extend throughout the cosmic ranks—the loci in which the perfection through the names is completed, the ultimate objective referred to by 'I created creation to be known'.

**Editorial decision:** **Historical-print correction:** I1876 printed p6 (PDF scan 10) reads المطلب الغائي, while B2007 digital reads المطلب الغائبّ. Prefer ‘ultimate objective’ for the edited text; preserve the B2007 form in apparatus. This is a real print-level difference, not a manuscript variant.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** true; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

**Historical print note:** I1876 original printed p6, scan 10, reading `والمطلب الغائي`; I1876 p6 visibly reads الغائي where B2007 digital reads الغائبّ. Prefer historic print for a purposive objective; no manuscript inferred.. Not a manuscript variant.

### CR001-015 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** فقوله : « كنت » يخبر عن تعيّن مسبوق بالإطلاق

**Reviewed English:** Thus 'I was' tells of a determination preceded by nondelimitation.

**Editorial decision:** Priority of rank is explicated later, not of time.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-016 · revise

**Arabic selected excerpt:** فإن مفهوم تاء المتكلّم عن نفسه يؤذن بتعيّنه في نفسه وظهوره حالة تكلّمه تعيّنا وظهورا

**Reviewed English:** The first-person tāʾ, by which the speaker speaks of himself, implies his determination in himself and his manifestation when speaking, as determination and manifestation.

**Editorial decision:** More literal account of tāʾ al-mutakallim than the earlier compressed 'first-person marker' paraphrase.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-017 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** أما قوله : « كنزا مخفيّا » ينبئ عن غيبه وإطلاقه بحيث لا يدخل تحت حكم معيّن يحكم عليه من حيث هذا الغيب والإطلاق

**Reviewed English:** By contrast, 'a hidden treasure' signifies His unseen character and nondelimitation, in which no determinate judgment can be applied to Him precisely insofar as He is unseen and undelimited.

**Editorial decision:** The qualification min ḥayth is crucial; not an unrestricted negation of divine predication.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-018 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** ولا تحت إحاطة به علما من حيث تعيّناته أصلا ، فإنّ مطلق الخفاء يحكم بذلك كلّه

**Reviewed English:** Nor can He be encompassed by knowledge through His determinations in this respect; absolute hiddenness entails all this.

**Editorial decision:** Scope of the negation is the Essence precisely as hidden and undelimited.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-001-p18-20.js`.

### CR001-019 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وحيث كان الخفاء وقع خبرا « لكنت » عرف سبق الإطلاق والغيب على ذلك التعيّن ، ولكن سبقا رتبيّا لا زمانيّا ، فإنه صحّ أنه ليس عند اللّه صباح ولا مساء

**Reviewed English:** Since hiddenness is predicated of 'I was', nondelimitation and the Unseen are understood to precede that determination—but by priority of rank, not priority in time; for there is neither morning nor evening with God.

**Editorial decision:** The source explicitly supplies sabqan rutbiyyan lā zamāniyyan.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وقوله : « فأحببت » يخبر عن ميل أصلي هو وصلة بين الخفاء والظهور المذكور

**Reviewed English:** 'Then I loved' tells of an original inclination linking the hiddenness and manifestation just described.

**Editorial decision:** Maḥabba is not a subsequent temporal event.

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وما ثمّ إلّا التعيّن الذي هو عين الوحدة ، فكان عينه

**Reviewed English:** There is nothing there except the determination that is identical with unity; the inclination is therefore identical with it.

**Reviewed English:** 'That I might be known' points to the manifestation of what had been hidden.

### CR001-023 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وقوله : « فخلقت الخلق لأعرف » يعرف أن تقدير المقدّرات علما ووجودا يعقب ذلك الميل ويترتّب على متعلّقه الذي هو الظهور الحاصل بذلك التعيّن

**Reviewed English:** 'So I created creation to be known' indicates that the determining of what is to be determined, in knowledge and in existence, follows that inclination and is ordered to what it seeks: the manifestation arising through that determination.

**Editorial decision:** This clause has علما ووجودا explicitly, distinct from the uncertain reading of عمليا below.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

### CR001-024 · corrected

**Arabic selected excerpt:** ليتحقّق كمال ذلك الظهور مفصّلا متميّزا ويشمل حكمه وأثره المقدّر والمقدّر تعقّبا ذاتيّا لا زمانيّا ، بل عمليّا ووجوديّا أيضا بالنسبة إلى المقدّر العالم الأزلي وعلمه الذاتي والصفاتي الأبدي

**Reviewed English:** So that the perfection of disclosure may be realized in articulated distinction, and its rule and effect may encompass the determiner and what is determined in essential rather than temporal succession—and also in knowledge and existence with respect to the eternal Determiner who knows, and His everlasting essential and attributive knowledge.

Correct the edited reading to ‘in knowledge and existence’, supported by the immediate wording about the eternal knower and His knowledge. No manuscript readings verified.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/19?lang=ar

## B2007 p20 — Digitized modern Arabic edited source (selected excerpts)

### CR001-029 · annotate

**Arabic selected excerpt:** بقوله تعالى : وَلا يُحِيطُونَ بِهِ عِلْماً [ طه : الآية 110 ] ، وبقوله : « كنت كنزا مخفيّا » ونحو ذلك

**Reviewed English:** —as in God's words, 'They do not encompass Him in knowledge' (Qurʾān 20:110), and the saying 'I was a hidden treasure', and similar expressions.

**Editorial decision:** Distinguish an actual Qurʾānic verse from the unattested 'hidden treasure' saying, though Farghānī lists both as divine report.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/20?lang=ar

### CR001-030 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** والوجه الثاني : انقطاع أكمل الإدراكات والعلوم وأوسع الشهودات والفهوم إليه واعترافه بالعجز والقصور والحيرة فيه

**Reviewed English:** The second way is for the most perfect apprehensions and sciences, and the widest witnessings and understandings, to come to their limit before Him and confess their incapacity, limitation and bewilderment concerning Him.

**Editorial decision:** Do not construe 'confession of incapacity' as ignorance of every divine name.

### CR001-031 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وبأن ما وراء غاية مدركه ونهاية مشهده بحار يحار في تيّارها فهم كل غائص واقف ، ويتيه في تيه كنهها وغور أسرارها علم كل خائض عارف

**Reviewed English:** They acknowledge that beyond the furthest object of apprehension and the last horizon of witnessing are seas whose currents bewilder every diver's understanding, and deserts whose hidden depths confound the knowledge of every knowing traveler.

**Editorial decision:** Preserves the two images, seas and trackless wilderness.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/20?lang=ar

### CR001-032 · retain

**Reviewed English:** These are the highest end and most worthy object of seeking; the Prophet indicated this in his supplication, 'I cannot enumerate the praise due to You.'

**Editorial decision:** The poetic plural 'these' refers back to inaccessible depths, not a positive list of predicates.

**Reviewed English:** That is, 'I cannot reach all that is in You'; and likewise in his supplication, 'or [a name] that You have reserved in the hidden mystery with You'.

**Editorial decision:** 'I cannot enumerate Your praise' appears in Ṣaḥīḥ Muslim 486; distinguish Farghānī's gloss from the hadith's words. The reserved names phrase comes from another supplication.

## B2007 p21 — Digitized modern Arabic edited source (selected excerpts)

### CR002-001 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** المتجلّي له من باب « كنت سمعه وبصره ، فبي يسمع وبي يبصر »

**Reviewed English:** —the one to whom disclosure occurs, in the sense of 'I become his hearing and sight, so by Me he hears and sees'.

**Editorial decision:** Continuation of B2007 p20; distinguish report from authorial conceptual explanation.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-002 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وفي السير المحبوبي وقرب الفرائض وتأخّر السلوك عن الجذبة وتقدم البقاء الأصلي على الفناء

**Reviewed English:** In the journey of the beloved and the nearness of obligatory acts, where traveling follows attraction and original subsistence precedes annihilation—

**Editorial decision:** A reversal of the order assigned to the ḥubbī journey at p20.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-003 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** حيث يتبيّن أن العبد المتجلّى له آلة لإدراك الحق المتجلّي من باب « فإن اللّه تعالى قال على لسان عبده : سمع اللّه تعالى لمن حمده »

**Reviewed English:** —it becomes clear that the servant who receives disclosure is the instrument of the self-disclosing Real's apprehension, in the sense of 'God speaks on His servant's tongue: God hears the one who praises Him.'

**Editorial decision:** Instrument-to-apprehender relation is reversed relative to the earlier sentence.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar

### CR002-004 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وعند انتهاء السيرين والجمع بين الحكمين ابتداء وانتهاء حيث يصح أن كل واحد منهما مدركا وآلة على التعاقب أو معا في حالة واحدة من باب وَما رَمَيْتَ إِذْ رَمَيْتَ وَلكِنَّ اللَّهَ رَمى

**Reviewed English:** At the completion of both journeys and their union in beginning and end, each can be the apprehender and the instrument, successively or together in one state, as in 'You did not throw when you threw, but God threw' (Qurʾān 8:17).

**Editorial decision:** The subject is the reciprocal grammatical distribution of apprehender and instrument, not equality of independent beings.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-005 · corrected

**Arabic selected excerpt:** فعلى كل حال يكون ذلك العلم والشهود والتجلّي والإدراك من حيث تعينه ومشيئته وعلمه المقدّس بذاته الأقدس تعالى وتقدّس من حيث واحديتها ، لا من حيث إطلاقها وأحديتها

**Reviewed English:** In every case, this knowing, witnessing, disclosure and apprehension concern His determination, His will and His sacred knowledge of His most holy Essence—exalted and sanctified is He—under the consideration of its oneness (wāḥidiyya), not under that of its nondelimitation and unicity (aḥadiyya).

**Editorial decision:** The older F007 excerpt omitted 'من حيث واحديتها'. The corrected B2007 source is confirmed; the phrase also appears in a later quotation in al-Fanārī's Miṣbāḥ al-uns (modern printed text p183), though this is reception testimony not an independent manuscript witness. Direct historical-print check: I1876 p7 (PDF scan 11) also contains من حيث واحديتها. This confirms the correction of the earlier project excerpt; it is not a manuscript variant.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** true; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

**Historical print note:** I1876 original printed p7, scan 11, reading `من حيث واحديتها`; I1876 p7 confirms the phrase B2007 has, previously omitted in a GNAMMA excerpt.. Not a manuscript variant.

### CR002-006 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** فإن إدراكه وشهوده من حيث تعيّنه وعلمه غير ممنوع ، بل موكول إلى مشيئته تعالى وتقدّس كما قال عزّ من قائل : وَلا يُحِيطُونَ بِشَيْءٍ مِنْ عِلْمِهِ إِلَّا بِما شاءَ

**Reviewed English:** For apprehending and witnessing Him under the consideration of His determination and knowledge is not prohibited; it is dependent upon His will, as He says, 'They encompass none of His knowledge except what He wills' (Qurʾān 2:255).

**Editorial decision:** The scope qualifier is essential to avoid treating the Essence's undelimitation as an object of determined knowledge.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-007 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** ثم إن وحدته التي انتشئت منها الأحديّة والواحدية هي عين ذاته لا صفة زائدة ولا نعت زائد عليها

**Reviewed English:** His unity, from which unicity and oneness derive, is identical with His Essence: it is neither an additional attribute nor an additional qualification.

**Editorial decision:** Do not identify two considerations with two distinct divine substances.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-008 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** لكن للوحدة اعتباران أصليّان أوليّان ، أحدهما : سقوط الاعتبارات عنها بالكلية ، وسمّي الذات أحدا بهذا الاعتبار ومتعلقة بطون الذات وإطلاقها وأزليّتها

**Reviewed English:** But unity admits two original, primary considerations. The first is the complete dropping away of considerations, under which the Essence is called One in unicity (aḥad), related to its hiddenness, nondelimitation and pre-eternity.

**Editorial decision:** Important to separate first determination (comprehensive intermediary) from aḥadiyya (one consideration).

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-009 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وعلى هذا يكون نسبة الاسم الأحد إلى السلب أحقّ من نسبته إلى الثبوت والإيجاب

**Reviewed English:** Thus the name aḥad is more properly related to negation than to establishment and affirmation.

**Editorial decision:** Metaphysical status of the negative consideration.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-010 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** والاعتبار الثاني : ثبوت الاعتبارات الغير المتناهية لها مع اندراجها فيها في أول رتبة الذات ، وتحقّق أكثر تفصيل تعيّناتها في ثاني الرتبة الآتي بيان كليهما عن قريب إن شاء اللّه تعالى

**Reviewed English:** The second consideration is the establishment of infinitely many considerations for unity, included within it at the first rank of the Essence, while most of their determinate differentiation is realized at the second rank, as both will shortly be explained.

**Editorial decision:** Do not read second-rank explicit differentiation back into the first-rank inclusion.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-011 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** كالنصفية والثلثية والربعية الثابتة والمندرجة في هذا الواحد المشهود ههنا ، التي ينتشىء منها الأعداد في المراتب الوجودية

**Reviewed English:** Like halfness, thirdness and quarterness, established and contained in the one considered here, from which numbers arise in existential ranks.

**Editorial decision:** Numerical analogy illustrates included relations, not arithmetic divisions within the Essence.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/21?lang=ar

### CR002-012 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وينتشىء من تلك الاعتبارات أعيان الاثنين والثلاثة والأربعة فيها ، كما يقال : الواحد نصف الاثنين ، وثلث الثلاثة ، وربع الأربعة وهلمّ جرّا

**Reviewed English:** And from these considerations arise the distinct two, three and four: the one is half of two, a third of three, a quarter of four, and so on.

**Editorial decision:** Keep ʿayn as determinate identity here, not the same sense as externally existing individual.

### CR002-013 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** والذات بهذا الاعتبار يسمّى واحدا اسما ثبوتيّا لا سلبيّا ، ولم تصح إضافة هذه الاعتبارات المذكورة إلى الاسم الأحد لا حقيقة ولا وضعا لغويّا

**Reviewed English:** Under this consideration the Essence is called wāḥid, an affirmative rather than a negative name. These considerations are not properly attributed to the name aḥad, either in reality or by linguistic convention.

## B2007 p22 — Digitized modern Arabic edited source (selected excerpts)

### CR002-014 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** الاعتبارات في أول رتبة الذات ثابتة للاسم الواحد الحقيقي ، ومندرجة فيه ثبوتا حقيقيّا واندراجا أصليّا

**Reviewed English:** These considerations at the first rank of the Essence are established for the truly One and included within it by real establishment and original inclusion.

**Editorial decision:** Source presents true thubūt but does not equate it with external existential actuality.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-015 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** كما أن أمثلتها وصورها ثابتة لهذا الواحد المشهود أثره لنا ههنا ، ومندرجة فيه غير قادحة في الرتبتين في صرافة هذا الواحد ، ولا منافية لها

**Reviewed English:** As their analogues and forms are established within the one whose effect is witnessed here, their inclusion at either rank neither impairs nor contradicts the simplicity of that one.

**Editorial decision:** The clause explicitly controls the number analogy's ontological limits.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-016 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** ومتعلق هذا الاعتبار الواحدي ظهور الذات ووجودها وأبديّتها ؛ كما أن متعلق الاعتبار الأحدي بطونها وأزليّتها

**Reviewed English:** The consideration of oneness concerns the Essence's manifestation, existence and perpetuity; the consideration of unicity concerns its hiddenness and pre-eternity.

**Editorial decision:** Preserve the parallel between ẓuhūr / buṭūn and abad / azal.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-017 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** ولا مغايرة ولا غيرية بين هذين الاعتبارين ، ولا بين كل اعتبار واعتبار من اعتبارات الواحدية في أول رتبة الذات الآتي بيانه

**Reviewed English:** There is neither differentiation nor otherness between these two considerations, nor between one consideration of oneness and another at the first rank of the Essence.

**Editorial decision:** Distinguish mughāyara and ghayriyya as both negated, not just simple sameness.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-018 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** لأن المغايرة والغيرية من أحكام الكثرة والمنافاة الثابتة بينها وبين الوحدة ، ولا كثرة ثمة تنافي الوحدة ، بل تلك الوحدة هي المنشئة لكل وحدة وكثرة مفهومة ههنا متغايرة عندنا

**Reviewed English:** For difference and otherness are judgments of multiplicity and its opposition to unity. There, no multiplicity exists that opposes unity; that unity is the source of every unity and multiplicity understood here as different.

**Editorial decision:** This does not by itself negate real plurality in subsequent manifested ranks.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-019 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** ولا يفهم ارتفاع هذه المغايرة في تلك الحضرة إلّا من ارتفع حالا ومقاما عن التقيّد بالمراتب المقيّدة بأحكام الكثرة والمقيّدة لأربابها بها

**Reviewed English:** None understands the removal of such difference in that Presence unless he has risen, in state and station, beyond restriction by ranks determined by judgments of multiplicity and restricting their occupants through them.

**Editorial decision:** Explicit link between ontological rank and transformed apprehension.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/22?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-020 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** ولارتفاع حكم هذه المغايرة في الرتبة الأولى حكم بها بعض أكابر المحقّقين من أهل اللّه بأن الواحد الأحد اسم واحد مركّب ؛ كبعلبك ومعديكرب

**Reviewed English:** Because the judgment of difference is absent at the first rank, some eminent realized masters hold that al-wāḥid al-aḥad is one compound name, like Baʿlabakk or Maʿdīkarib.

**Editorial decision:** Grammatical examples are genuine proper-name compounds; not a third ontological rank.

### CR002-021 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** ثم اعلم أن هذه الاعتبارات الثابتة للواحد ثمة ، والمندرجة فيه في أول رتبة الذات المحكوم عليها فيها بأنها عين الذات

**Reviewed English:** Know, further, that these considerations established for the One and included within it at the first rank of the Essence are judged there to be the Essence itself.

**Editorial decision:** Differentiation at later ranks must not be inserted into this first-rank claim.

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

### CR002-022 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** بحيث إن كل ما يضاف إلى الذات يضاف إلى كلّ واحد منها من الجمعية والاشتمال على الكلّ في هذه الرتبة الأولى بحسبها

**Reviewed English:** Thus what is attributed to the Essence under the aspect of comprehensiveness and containing the whole is likewise attributed to each of the considerations at this rank.

**Editorial decision:** The qualifier 'at this rank' is essential.

### CR002-023 · retain

### CR002-025 · retain

### CR002-031 · corrected

**Arabic selected excerpt:** إلّا أنه ربما يكون في الحضرة الغيبية الأزليّة نحو هذه الكلّيات والجزئيّات أمور باطنة وأحوال مستورة لم تتعيّن بعد لا في الرتبة الثانية ولا في اللّوح المحفوظ

**Reviewed English:** Yet in the eternal unseen Presence there may perhaps be, regarding these universals and particulars, hidden things and concealed states not yet determined either at the second rank or in the Preserved Tablet.

**Editorial decision:** PHOTOGRAPHIC PRINT-TO-PRINT DIFFERENCE I1876 printed p8/scan12: سوى أنه ربما يكون; B2007 printed p23: إلّا أنه ربما يكون. Prefer I1876 connective provisionally; the modal ربما and the uncertain status of undetermined affairs must remain. Medieval witnesses not yet collated.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** true; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

**Historical print note:** I1876 original printed p8, scan 12, reading `سوى أنه ربما يكون`; Original-resolution historical I1876 p8 has سوى أنه, while B2007 p23 reads إلا أنه. Both are exceptive; adopt earlier print provisionally, with the Beirut reading preserved, without changing the modal nuance of ربما. No manuscript compared.. Not a manuscript variant.

### CR002-032 · crux

**Arabic selected excerpt:** فلم تعلم ولم يحكم بشيء منها إلّا بعد تعيّنها ووقوعها في المراتب الوجودية بالتدريج مع الآنات

**Reviewed English:** Accordingly they are not known or judged until their determination and occurrence in existential ranks, progressively with successive instants.

**Editorial decision:** Historical print agreement now established at I1876 pp8–9 (scans12–13): the source really has فلم تعلم ولم يحكم بشيء منها إلا بعد تعينها. Preserve the claim exactly; it remains a difficult question about determinacy and knowledge, not evidence for an invented manuscript emendation.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/23?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** printed-digital-Arabic-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** true; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-002-p21-23.js`.

**Historical print note:** I1876 original printed p8–9, scan 12–13, reading `فلم تعلم ولم يحكم بشيء منها إلا بعد تعينها`; Historic 1876 printing p8 end and p9 beginning preserve the difficult expression. This is a bounded print agreement; theological interpretation and manuscript text remain unresolved.. Not a manuscript variant.

**Reviewed English:** at the seventh rank of speech's inward depths.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar

### CR003-013 · annotate

**Arabic selected excerpt:** إن للقرآن ظهرا وبطنّا ، وفي بطنه بطن إلى سبعة أبطن

**Reviewed English:** The Qurʾān has an outward and an inward, and within its inward a further inward, to seven inward depths.

**Editorial decision:** Reported saying appears in Farghānī's authorial text, but the modern editor states this exact wording was not located. Do not mark as authenticated prophetic ḥadīth.

**Reviewed English:** as one of us speaks inwardly without an outward letter or audible sound.

**Editorial decision:** Farghānī explicitly says the comparison is analogical and excludes physical utterance.

**Reviewed English:** the Essence's receptive inclination toward hearing and listening to this discourse is in truth the effect of that love.

**Editorial decision:** Inclination, love, and hearing here name principial relational considerations, not change caused by lack in the Essence.

**Reviewed English:** the Essence's beholding the light of its beauty, which is identical with its oneness, manifestation, and manifestation of its considerations.

**Editorial decision:** Keep the repeated 'manifestation' rather than flattening the subject–object relation.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar

### CR003-017 · revise

**Arabic selected excerpt:** ليميط خمار غلبة حكم جلال الغيب والبطون عن الجمال والظهور

**Reviewed English:** so as to remove the veil of the preponderance of the majesty of the Unseen and hiddenness from beauty and manifestation.

**Editorial decision:** The digital source writes ليميط without vocalization; the 'veil' is figurative and must not be literalized.

### CR003-018 · crux

**Arabic selected excerpt:** بتغليب الرحمة وسبقها على أنها غايات حكم الغضب عارجا لا نازلا

**Reviewed English:** through the preponderance and precedence of mercy, in contrast to the furthest limit of the judgment called anger, upward rather than downward.

**Editorial decision:** I1876 p10 / scan14 now confirms the bounded concluding phrase حكم الغضب عارجا لا نازلا. This historic PRINT agreement does not decide the syntactic attachment of على أنها غايات and does not justify smoothing the whole sentence.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/25?lang=ar

**Historical print note:** I1876 original printed p10, scan 14, reading `حكم الغضب عارجا لا نازلا`; Narrow ending contrast is photographed in I1876 p10/scan14 and B2007 p25. Earlier على أنها غايات remains syntactically unsettled.. Not a manuscript variant.

### CR003-019 · retain

**Reviewed English:** For manifestation and non-manifestation stood on an equal footing with respect to what this primordial receptivity entails.

**Editorial decision:** This is a logical/principial contrast, not a chronological episode when God lacked manifestation.

**Reviewed English:** The Essence found itself through this self-disclosure, which itself is knowledge and witnessing, under the consideration of the mirror already mentioned.

**Editorial decision:** 'Mirror' is identical with Essence under a respect, not a second existing object.

## B2007 p26 — Digitized modern Arabic edited source (selected excerpts)

### CR003-022 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** والسمع هو قبول ذلك المعنى ظاهرا أو باطنا

**Reviewed English:** Hearing is the reception of that meaning, outwardly or inwardly.

**Editorial decision:** Defines a faculty relative to meaning and its manifestation, not exclusively bodily audition.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-003-p24-29.js`.

### CR003-023 · revise

**Arabic selected excerpt:** والبصر هو قبول ظهوره بما هو ظاهر من القابل منه

**Reviewed English:** Sight is receptivity to the meaning's appearing in whatever becomes manifest to its recipient.

**Editorial decision:** The printed Arabic is syntactically dense. The object is the appearance of the meaning; further historical witnesses may refine referents.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-003-p24-29.js`.

### CR003-024 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** والقوة تمكن مما يطلب ظهوره

**Reviewed English:** Power is the capacity for the realization of what seeks to appear.

**Editorial decision:** Quwwa is explicitly defined by the intended appearance, not bare mechanical force.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-003-p24-29.js`.

### CR003-025 · crux

**Arabic selected excerpt:** فلفظ وكلي بي لسان محدث

**Reviewed English:** My utterance—and my whole being is a tongue speaking through me.

**Editorial decision:** First Ibn al-Fāriḍ hemistich in Beirut. Collateral poetic reading: B2007 p26 gives ولحظ ... عين لعبرة, whereas a separate published Tāʾiyya in the Qayṣarī commentary (verse 545) and Wikisource read لعبرتي. These are parallel poem texts, NOT independent Farghānī manuscript witnesses.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-003-p24-29.js`.

### CR003-026 · revise

**Arabic selected excerpt:** معاني صفات ما ورى اللّبس أثبتت

**Reviewed English:** The meanings of the attributes were established beyond their clothing in multiplicity.

**Editorial decision:** Lubs ('putting on' or 'clothing') is the metaphor for differentiation of what is not yet distinct at the first rank; previous 'beyond the clothing of appearance' under-specified this.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-003-p24-29.js`.

### CR003-027 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** صفات متغايرة في الرتبة الثانية بحسبها وحكمها

**Reviewed English:** attributes distinct from one another at the second rank according to that rank's law.

**Editorial decision:** Explicit differentiation occurs at second rank; avoid transferring independent multiplicity to the Essence at first rank.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-003-p24-29.js`.

### CR003-028 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** بعد أن لم تكن في تلك الرتبة الأولى صفات ، بل اعتبارات غير متميّزة ولا متغايرة فيها

**Reviewed English:** though at that first rank they were not attributes but considerations neither distinct nor mutually divergent.

**Editorial decision:** Critical distinction: shūʾūn/considerations at first rank vs distinct attributes at second rank.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-003-p24-29.js`.

### CR003-029 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وباطنها الأول : أن تكون هذه الصفات مضافة إلى نفسه

**Reviewed English:** The first inward level is the attribution of these qualities to the individual's soul.

**Editorial decision:** The 'first inward' degree follows their outward manifestation in bodily faculties, and so is not the initial divine self-disclosure.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-span-checked-v1; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-003-p24-29.js`.

### CR003-030 · revise

**Arabic selected excerpt:** بظاهر العقل المعيشي المقيّد بأمور دنيوية

**Reviewed English:** by the outward appearance of practical intellect restricted to worldly concerns.

**Editorial decision:** ʿAql maʿīshī is livelihood-oriented intellect; 'practical intellect' here is contextual and must not be mistaken for the whole Aristotelian practical intellect.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/26?lang=ar

## B2007 p27 — Digitized modern Arabic edited source (selected excerpts)

### CR003-031 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وباطنها الثاني : ما يضاف إلى نفسه من حيث عقله المنوّر بنور الشرع والإيمان باللّه

**Reviewed English:** The second inward level pertains to the soul insofar as its intellect is illuminated by revealed law and faith in God.

**Editorial decision:** Law and faith illuminate intellect, enabling reorientation from world to afterlife; do not collapse into generic philosophical insight.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar

**Reviewed English:** The third inward level pertains to the individual's immaterial spiritual spirit.

**Editorial decision:** Preserve the doubled expression rūḥuhu al-rūḥāniyya; it differentiates this level from ordinary psychical faculty.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar

**Editorial decision:** That distinctness belongs to a cosmic level; no reason to infer independent divine existence.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/27?lang=ar

**Editorial decision:** ʿAynī is actual/concrete rather than 'eye-like'; at this level the same senses are interpreted as disclosures of existential reality.

**Reviewed English:** delimited by it and manifesting its rulings in cosmic ranks as spirit, image, and sense.

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وسعني قلب عبدي

**Reviewed English:** The heart of My servant encompassed Me.

**Reviewed English:** the inward reality of every divine and cosmic reality.

**Reviewed English:** it permeates each in its entirety, such that it is divine in divine realities and cosmic in cosmic realities.

**Editorial decision:** Do not translate pervasion as literal local diffusion or confusion of creator and creation.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/29?lang=ar

**Reviewed English:** Some call it the Supreme Intermediary, encompassing all intermediaries and being their pervasive principle.

**Editorial decision:** Attribution is to 'some'; not an equation automatically accepted across all Akbarī authors.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/29?lang=ar

**Reviewed English:** Revelation alludes to it through the station of 'or nearer,' the inward reality of 'the distance of two bows'.

**Editorial decision:** Qurʾān 53:9 is interpreted through Farghānī's metaphysical hermeneutics; distinguish Qurʾānic wording from that interpretation.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/29?lang=ar

**Reviewed English:** that is, the two bows of unity and multiplicity, or receptivity and activity, or necessity and possibility.

**Editorial decision:** Three alternative relational axes are illustrative of a comprehensive intermediary, not interchangeable logical predicates in every context.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/29?lang=ar

**Reviewed English:** The inward reality of this station is 'or nearer': here no trace of differentiation or multiplicity remains.

**Editorial decision:** The prior 'two bows' station retains a subtle distinction, whereas 'or nearer' excludes even that within the comprehensive circle.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/29?lang=ar

**Reviewed English:** Some designate it the Aḥmadian Reality, established at the heart of intermediacy and just proportion.

**Editorial decision:** Aḥmadian is a specific Muhammadan metaphysical symbol, not a synonym to be substituted without qualification for the other named realities.

Adopt masculine as provisional edited reading; no photographed MS variants.. Not a manuscript variant.

**Reviewed English:** The first thing God created was my light.

**Editorial decision:** Quoted within Farghānī's argument; independently established prophetic authenticity is lacking. Keep authorial citation without endorsement.

**Reviewed English:** The first self-disclosure includes perfection, whose reality is the obtaining of what ought to be, as it ought to be.

### CR004-001 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** إلى شهود الأغيار في مراتبهم وتقيّدهم بها

**Reviewed English:** —to witnessing other things within their own ranks and their determination by those ranks.

**Editorial decision:** Completes the final sentence from p29. Printed-page boundaries are not sentence or conceptual boundaries.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/30?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-004-p30-36.js`.

### CR004-002 · revise

**Reviewed English:** and to everything's being one reality relative to the witnessing of the One Real in unicity.

**Editorial decision:** The distinction is between the cosmos' distinct witnessing and a principial witnessing; not an assertion that created entities are unqualifiedly God.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/30?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-004-p30-36.js`.

**Reviewed English:** The second division of perfection is differentiated perfection through the names.

**Editorial decision:** Marks the contrast with the first, essentially comprehensive perfection.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/30?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-004-p30-36.js`.

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وذلك يكون بشرط شيء ، بل أشياء

**Reviewed English:** This occurs on condition of something—indeed, several conditions.

**Editorial decision:** Name-related perfection is conditioned by modes and loci; essential perfection is not.

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-004-p30-36.js`.

**Reviewed English:** provided that witnessing occurs through a manifestation of a universal affair encompassing its members in actuality: the real Perfect Human.

**Editorial decision:** Do not generalize 'the Perfect Human' as merely the sum of humans. The Arabic emphasizes universality and actuality.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/30?lang=ar

**Reviewed English:** and the consideration of knowledge, whose reality is the appearance of the very reality, so that its effect is present in the one to whom it appears.

**Editorial decision:** ʿAyn occurs twice in different syntactic relations; a flat 'object appears' translation hides the self-manifestation terminology.

**Reviewed English:** In the ranks of the cosmos only creation appears; in the ranks of the Real only the Real appears.

**Editorial decision:** Preserve the two predicative standpoints; avoid denying divine causal action in the cosmos.

**Reviewed English:** None knows, witnesses, or sees the Real except the Real Himself or through Him.

**Editorial decision:** Alternative 'through Him' is in the Arabic and indispensable for the experiential register.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/31?lang=ar

**Arabic selected excerpt:** فكان متعلّقا بمعلوم واحد

**Reviewed English:** It thus relates to one thing known.

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-004-p30-36.js`.

### CR004-014 · annotate

**Reviewed English:** Its expression, at this first rank, is transitive to one object.

**Editorial decision:** Farghānī makes an unusual verbal-transitivity analogy: it is his own grammatical illustration, not a general rule about the verb ʿalima; explicate with caution.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/31?lang=ar

**Reviewed English:** Knowledge at the completion of the second rank has real multiplicity and a collective relative unity in respect of its known contents.

**Editorial decision:** Do not ascribe multiplicity to the undelimited Essence without the second-rank qualifier.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/31?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-004-p30-36.js`.

**Reviewed English:** It therefore has real unity and relational multiplicity.

**Editorial decision:** Key register: unity of wujūd as manifested names, versus real diversity in cosmic modes.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/31?lang=ar

**Record-grade:** modern-printed-digital-selected-locus-checked; **historical print independently seen for this record** false; **manuscript independently seen** false. **Constituent file:** `data/critical-pass-004-p30-36.js`.

**Reviewed English:** As regards the reality of existence and determination itself, it is identical with the Essence.

This rejects an unsupported conjectural عين at print level but does not resolve syntactic reference or prove a medieval MS reading.. Not a manuscript variant.

### CR005-018 · crux

**Arabic selected excerpt:** كان في عماء ما فوقه هواء ، وما تحته هواء

**Reviewed English:** He was in ʿamāʾ; the following phrase concerning air above and below remains syntactically disputed.

**Editorial decision:** The Arabic phrase has competing interpretations: mā as negation (no air above/below) versus relative/interrogative or a variant without mā. Classical transmission itself varies. Do not silently impose one vocalization; see al-Tirmidhī 3109, Ibn Qutayba Taʾwīl mukhtalif al-ḥadīth, and critical hadith notes.

**Source:** https://ablibrary.net/book_content/b/9606/41?lang=ar

**Reviewed English:** This second self-disclosure in outward, concrete existence pervades the Cloud-like Presence.

**Editorial decision:** The second existential manifestation is an outward disclosure; avoid treating ʿamāʾ as a merely meteorological cloud.

## B2007 p61 — DERIVATIVE secondary transcription (not independent witness)

### CR007-001 · retain

**Arabic selected excerpt:** فذلك الإيثار يكون بالوجود أولا ، ثم بالبقاء ثانيا ، ثم بنفخ الروح والحياة ثالثا

**Reviewed English:** Hence this meaning occurs most often in intensive forms: al-Ghaffār indicating generality and al-Ghafūr multiplicity.

**Editorial decision:** Older English 'universality of repeated covering' for al-Ghaffār risks collapsing تعميما (generality) into تعديدا (multiplicity).

### CR007-036 · revise

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وهو - أعني ذلك الاسم - مليكه وسيّده والقريب اللازم له وممدّه بالوجود مع الآنات بالخلق الجديد دائما

**Reviewed English:** That name is his sovereign, master, enduring near presence, and supplier of being at successive instants through continual new creation.

### CR007-039 · annotate

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وأما الحكم الخاص الذي للربوبية هو ما ذكرنا أن كل ما تعيّن وجوده أولا من حضرة اسم كان ذلك الاسم ربّه الخاص

### CR007-044 · annotate

**Arabic selected excerpt:** وأما نبيّنا محمد صلى اللّه عليه وسلم فله المنهل الأعلى ، وهو التجلّي الأول الذي هو نوره أولا ، وربّه ثانيا

**Reviewed English:** Our Prophet Muḥammad has the supreme spring: the first self-disclosure, his light first and his Lord in a second regard.

## Required restart: primary witness collation

All four foundations plus supplementary preface have **selected** checks across all B2007 page labels. This does NOT establish an Arabic critical edition, line-by-line proof of the 128-page English draft, or independent witnesses for the textual cruxes. Work next on full actual photographed B2007/I1876 pages, locate actual readable manuscripts C1107/R670/K273/F3966/Y706, establish folio concordance and continuous Arabic lemmas, check each English clause, and retain variants with independent image evidence. Verse commentary beyond p145 remains a separate translation project.
